# Claude's triage (2026-10-15)

Codex reached its usage limit after covering every file: 267 kept, 43 missing, 54 unsure.
Claude checked the priority list against the code. Fixed in the commit that names A59, each
with a test porting iOS's assertion and a mutation check: **#2** (a success from an ended
session clears no draft), **#5** (the Inbox pill and filter mark read the page on screen),
**#6** (an unknown chapter count is neither baselined nor marked as zero), and Codex's
unfinished History note (the Clear History confirmation and its "cleared" state belong to the
session that showed them). **#1, #3 and #4** and the other missing rows go to brief 3fm.

# Priority list — missing rules worth fixing first

1. Fence comments and Inbox destinations to their opening session: Android reopens the original focused destination after an account switch and permits authenticated comment-read failures to fall back to public HTML (`CommentsAccountTransitionTests`; `CommentsViewModel.kt:245–276`, `AO3CommentRepository.kt:48–74`).
2. Preserve account-bound comment recovery on stale success: Android clears the sending account's draft variants before checking whether that session is still current (`CommentsAccountTransitionTests.staleSuccessfulSubmitCannotClearOrCloseTheNewAccountsComposer`; `CommentsViewModel.kt:822–825`).
3. Correct comment list/divider/link/spoiler transforms: one `<li>` wraps all selected lines; a divider precedes selected words; link text stays selected instead of putting the caret in href (`CommentMarkupTests`; `CommentMarkup.kt:86–91,148–160`).
4. Qualify account counts by session generation and preserve AO3's exact bookmark total: cache callers pass only username and single-page parsed work rows become exact totals (`AO3AccountListCountsTests`; `AccountListRepository.kt:57–63`, `AccountViewModel.kt:174`).
5. Keep Inbox filter pills on the filter of the rows actually shown when a request fails: filterValues changes before the read, and the failure path never restores it (`AO3InboxPaginationFailureTests`; `AccountInboxViewModel.kt:251–258,166–176`).
6. Do not baseline or mark unknown subscription chapter counts as zero: sparse rows can overwrite real watermarks or later badge every chapter as new (`SubscriptionWatermarkTests`; `SubscriptionWatermarks.kt:78–82,97–106`).

Ordering is maintained as remaining paths are checked; full evidence follows in each test table.

# A59 — Writing, Comments and AO3 account screens

Read-only static audit. Android evidence is read with `git show a0bddb6d:<path>` from `/Users/cidy02/kudos-android-lane` (its working HEAD has advanced). iOS evidence is from `/Users/cidy02/kudos-ios-polish/KudosTests/` and `kudos-ao3-reader/`. No builds, tests, authentication or AO3 requests are performed. Only this report is written; TASKS.md is untouched. Binding policy: `docs/AO3_NETWORKING_POLICY.md`.

Paths in tables: iOS paths are relative to `/Users/cidy02/kudos-ios-polish/`; Android paths are relative to `/Users/cidy02/kudos-android-lane/` at **a0bddb6d**. A Kotlin quote establishes static behavior, not runtime verification. **unsure** means the entire test guarantee is not established by both sides. Related Android tests are named where found; none were run.

when (failure) { &quot;refusal&quot; -&gt; setup.client.postResponse = seriesResponse(422, &quot;&lt;div id=&#x27;error&#x27;&gt;&lt;ul&gt;&lt;li&gt;Removal is invalid&lt;/li&gt;&lt;/ul&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&quot;) &quot;unconfirmed&quot; -&gt; setup.client.applyOrder = false &quot;last work&quot; -&gt; { val doc = org.jsoup.Jsoup.parse(setup.client.manage) doc.select(&quot;#sortable_series_list li&quot;).drop(1).forEach { it.remove() } setup.client.manage = doc.outerHtml() } &quot;session&quot; -&gt; setup.auth.logout() } model.remove(draft.works.first()) assertEquals(draft, model.state.value.form) assertEquals(if (failure in listOf(&quot;last work&quot;, &quot;session&quot;)) 0 else 1, setup.client.posts.size) val reason = when (failure) { &quot;refusal&quot; -&gt; &quot;Removal is invalid&quot; &quot;unconfirmed&quot; -&gt; AO3CollectionFields.UNCONFIRMED &quot;last work&quot; -&gt; &quot;It is the series&#x27; last work on AO3, and AO3 deletes a series with its last work.&quot; else -&gt; null } // A removal AO3 may have carried out is not called &quot;not removed&quot;. assertEquals(reason?.let { if (it == AO3CollectionFields.UNCONFIRMED) it else &quot;First Light was not removed. $it&quot; }</code> |

text[end - 1] == &#x27;\n&#x27; val closesLine = end &lt; text.length &amp;&amp; text[end] == &#x27;\n&#x27; prefix = selected + (if (opensLine) &quot;&quot; else &quot;\n&quot;) + &quot;&lt;hr&gt;&quot; + (if (closesLine) &quot;&quot; else &quot;\n&quot;) body = &quot;&quot;; suffix = &quot;&quot; } &quot;br&quot; -&gt; { prefix = &quot;&lt;br&gt;&quot;; body = selected; suffix = &quot;&quot; } &quot;ul&quot;, &quot;ol&quot; -&gt; { val items = selected.split(&#x27;\n&#x27;).map { it.trim { ch -&gt; ch == &#x27;\t&#x27; &#124;&#124; Character.getType(ch) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR.toInt() } } .filter { it.isNotEmpty() }.map { &quot;&lt;li&gt;$it&lt;/li&gt;&quot; } if (items.isEmpty()) { prefix = &quot;&lt;$tag&gt;\n&lt;li&gt;&quot;; body = &quot;&quot;; suffix = &quot;&lt;/li&gt;\n&lt;/$tag&gt;&quot; } else { prefix = &quot;&lt;$tag&gt;\n&quot;; body = items.joinToString(&quot;\n&quot;); body = selected; suffix = &quot;&lt;/a&gt;&quot; } &quot;details&quot; -&gt; { prefix = &quot;&lt;details&gt;&lt;summary&gt;&quot;; body = &quot;&quot;; suffix = &quot;&lt;/summary&gt;$selected&lt;/details&gt;&quot; { if (revision == checkpointedRevision) return null checkpointedRevision = revision val value = takeText() if (Normalizer.normalize(value, Normalizer.Form.NFC) == Normalizer.normalize(checkpointedText, Normalizer.Form.NFC)) return null checkpointedText = value return value }  /** Explicit commands only. Idle/background/memory snapshots never finish composing text. */ fun commitComposition() { view.onCreateInputConnection(EditorInfo())?.finishComposingText() }  fun insert(tag: String, link: String = &quot;&quot;) { commitComposition() val start = minOf(view.selectionStart, view.selectionEnd).coerceAtLeast(0) val end = maxOf(view.selectionStart, view.selectionEnd).coerceAtLeast(start) val insertion = WritingMarkup.insertion(tag, view.text, start, end, link) ?: return // Outside a batch: Editor&#x27;s UndoInputFilter makes this one separate undoable change. view.text.replace(start, end, insertion.text) view.setSelection(start + insertion.contentOffset, start + insertion.contentOffset + insertion.contentLength) view.requestFocus() view.post { view.bringPointIntoView(view.selectionEnd) } }  /** Set text is an undoable replacement, including Restore. Never call EditText.setText here. */ fun setText(value: String) { commitComposition() view.text.replace(0, view.text.length, value) view.setSelection(value.length) view.post { view.bringPointIntoView(view.selectionEnd) } }  fun undo() { commitComposition(); view.onTextContextMenuItem(android.R.id.undo) } fun redo() { commitComposition(); view.onTextContextMenuItem(android.R.id.redo) } fun pastePlainText() { commitComposition(); view.onTextContextMenuItem(android.R.id.pasteAsPlainText) }</code><br>Native text snapshots preserve raw markup; -&gt; elements += child to false &quot;li&quot; -&gt; elements += child to true else -&gt; collect(child) } } wrapLoose(root) collect(root) if (elements.isEmpty()) elements += root to false State.Rendered(elements.mapNotNull { (element, listItem) -&gt; it.text.contains(&quot;hidden&quot;) }) assertTrue((WritingBufferPreview.state(&quot;&quot;) as WritingBufferPreview.State.Rendered).blocks.isEmpty()) }  @Test fun thePreviewShowsTextTypedOutsideABlockTag() { fun texts(html: String) = (WritingBufferPreview.state(html) as WritingBufferPreview.State.Rendered) .blocks.map { block -&gt; | Test and reader guarantee | iOS assertion | Verdict | Android evidence and consequence |
|---|---|---|---|
| <code>parsesTheNoticeDateAndSkipsABlurbWithout</code><br>Parses The Notice Date And Skips A Blurb Without. | KudosTests/DraftExpiryTests.swift:58 <code>#expect(dates[41_234_567] == DateComponents(year: 2026, month: 10, day: 5))</code><br>KudosTests/DraftExpiryTests.swift:59 <code>#expect(dates[41_234_568] == nil)</code><br>KudosTests/DraftExpiryTests.swift:60 <code>#expect(dates.count == 1)</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/writing/AO3Drafts.kt:33 <code>fun parseDraftDeletionDates(html: String): Map&lt;Long, LocalDate&gt; = buildMap { for (blurb in Jsoup.parse(html).select(&quot;li.work.blurb&quot;)) { if (!blurb.id().startsWith(&quot;work_&quot;)) continue val id = blurb.id().removePrefix(&quot;work_&quot;).toLongOrNull() ?: continue val notice = blurb.selectFirst(&quot;p.caution.notice&quot;) ?: continue val day = notice.selectFirst(&quot;span.date&quot;)?.text()?.trim()?.toIntOrNull() ?: continue val year = notice.selectFirst(&quot;span.year&quot;)?.text()?.trim()?.toIntOrNull() ?: continue val monthName = notice.selectFirst(&quot;abbr.month&quot;)?.attr(&quot;title&quot;)?.trim() ?: continue val month = months.indexOf(monthName) + 1 if (month == 0) continue // Calendar.date(from:) on iOS normalizes the date components too. val date = runCatching { LocalDate.of(year, month, 1).plusDays(day.toLong() - 1) }.getOrNull() if (date != null) put(id, date) }</code><br>Expiry comes only from AO3’s notice, never the work’s revised date. |
| <code>daysLeftCountsWholeDaysAndNeverGoesNegative</code><br>Days Left Counts Whole Days And Never Goes Negative. | KudosTests/DraftExpiryTests.swift:65 <code>#expect(DraftExpiry.daysLeft(until: deletion, now: date(2026, 10, 5), calendar: calendar) == 0)</code><br>KudosTests/DraftExpiryTests.swift:66 <code>#expect(DraftExpiry.daysLeft(until: deletion, now: date(2026, 10, 4, hour: 23), calendar: calendar) == 1)</code><br>KudosTests/DraftExpiryTests.swift:67 <code>#expect(DraftExpiry.daysLeft(until: deletion, now: date(2026, 10, 9), calendar: calendar) == 0)</code><br>KudosTests/DraftExpiryTests.swift:68 <code>#expect(DraftExpiry.daysLeft(until: deletion, now: date(2026, 9, 28), calendar: calendar) == 7)</code><br>KudosTests/DraftExpiryTests.swift:70 <code>#expect(DraftExpiry.daysLeft(until: incomplete, now: date(2026, 10, 1), calendar: calendar) == nil)</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/writing/AO3Drafts.kt:54 <code>object DraftExpiry { const val noticeOffsetDays = 29L enum class Tone(val hue: Double) { Red(0.0), Orange(0.08), Mint(0.45) }  fun daysLeft(deletion: LocalDate, clock: Clock): Int = daysLeft(deletion, LocalDate.now(clock)) fun daysLeft(deletion: LocalDate, today: LocalDate): Int = ChronoUnit.DAYS.between(today, deletion).coerceIn(0, Int.MAX_VALUE.toLong()).toInt() fun createdDate(deletion: LocalDate): LocalDate = deletion.minusDays(noticeOffsetDays) fun chipText(days: Int): String = when { days &lt;= 0 -&gt; &quot;Last day&quot; days == 1 -&gt; &quot;1 day left&quot; else -&gt; &quot;$days days left&quot;</code><br>android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/writing/AO3Drafts.kt:73 <code>fun tally(drafts: AO3DraftsPage, today: LocalDate): String { val page = drafts.page val expiring = page.works.count { work -&gt; drafts.deletionDates[work.id]?.let { daysLeft(it, today) &lt;= 7 } == true } if (page.totalPages &gt; 1) { return &quot;page ${page.currentPage} of ${page.totalPages}&quot; + if (expiring &gt; 0) &quot; · $expiring expiring this week on this page&quot; else &quot;&quot; } val count = page.works.size return (if (count == 1) &quot;1 draft&quot; else &quot;$count drafts&quot;) + if (expiring &gt; 0) &quot; · $expiring expiring this week&quot; else &quot;&quot;</code><br>LocalDate prevents partial date inputs; whole-day arithmetic clamps expired drafts to zero and the tally uses <=7 days. |

AO3Result.Success)?.value?.totalPages ?: 1 if (last &gt; 1) page(target, last, forceRefresh) else first } } ensureActive() // Try Again and pull to refresh are for the page that was asked for, which for the // order&#x27;s start is only known now. if (page == null &amp;&amp; result is AO3Result.Success) lastPage = result.value.currentPage present(target, result)</code><br>Switching opens a new load and cancels the old loadJob; ensureActive blocks an old read completion before it presents. |

newIdentity != null &amp;&amp; now.generation - previous.generation in 0..1) { draftHandoff = viewModelScope.launch { saved?.join() draftStore?.move(oldIdentity, newIdentity) } } viewer = now composer = null pendingReplyId = null _composerPresented.value = false _replyTarget.value = null _editTarget.value = null _composerParent.value = null _draft.value = &quot;&quot; ## CommentsInitialIntentTests

| Test and reader guarantee | iOS assertion | Verdict | Android evidence and consequence |

| <code>nothingStagedIsNothingPending</code><br>Nothing Staged Is Nothing Pending. | KudosTests/AO3CollectionItemStagingTests.swift:37 <code>#expect(staging.pendingCount(for: [item()]) == 0)</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/account/AO3CollectionItems.kt:74 <code>fun forItem(item: AO3CollectionItem) = copy( creatorApproval = creatorApproval.takeIf { item.creatorEditable }, moderatorApproval = moderatorApproval.takeIf { item.moderatorEditable }, unrevealed = unrevealed.takeIf { item.unrevealedEditable }, anonymous = anonymous.takeIf { item.anonymousEditable }, remove = remove &amp;&amp; item.removeEditable )  fun changes(item: AO3CollectionItem): Boolean = remove &#124;&#124; (creatorApproval != null &amp;&amp; creatorApproval != item.creatorApproval) &#124;&#124; (moderatorApproval != null &amp;&amp; moderatorApproval != item.moderatorApproval) &#124;&#124; (unrevealed != null &amp;&amp; unrevealed != item.unrevealed) &#124;&#124; (anonymous != null &amp;&amp; anonymous != item.anonymous)</code><br>android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/account/AO3CollectionItems.kt:104 <code>/** Immutable staging keeps off-page drafts, but only visible, editable changes are submitted. */ data class AO3CollectionItemStaging(val drafts: Map&lt;Int, AO3CollectionItemDraft&gt; = emptyMap()) { fun shown(item: AO3CollectionItem) = (drafts[item.id] ?: AO3CollectionItemDraft(item.id)).forItem(item) fun pending(items: List&lt;AO3CollectionItem&gt;) = items.mapNotNull { item -&gt; drafts[item.id]?.forItem(item)?.takeIf { it.changes(item) } }.sortedBy { it.itemId } fun set(item: AO3CollectionItem, change: (AO3CollectionItemDraft) -&gt; AO3CollectionItemDraft) = copy(drafts = drafts + (item.id to change(shown(item)))) fun remove(item: AO3CollectionItem, removed: Boolean) = copy(drafts = drafts + (item.id to AO3CollectionItemDraft(item.id, remove = removed))) fun clear(ids: List&lt;Int&gt;) = copy(drafts = drafts - ids.toSet())</code><br>Staging uses each item id, drops disabled controls, compares against its own baseline, submits only visible changes in id order, replaces edits with removal and clears only submitted ids.<br>Android test (static evidence; not run): android/app/src/test/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/account/AO3CollectionItemsTest.kt:75 <code>@Test fun stagingRevertsBaselineExcludesDisabledAndOffPageAndRemovalReplacesEdits() { val item = page().items.first() var staging = AO3CollectionItemStaging().set(item) { it.copy(moderatorApproval = AO3CollectionItemApproval.Approved) } assertEquals(1, staging.pending(listOf(item)).size) assertTrue(staging.pending(emptyList()).isEmpty()) staging = staging.set(item) { it.copy(moderatorApproval = item.moderatorApproval) } assertTrue(staging.pending(listOf(item)).isEmpty()) staging = staging.set(item) { it.copy(creatorApproval = AO3CollectionItemApproval.Rejected) } assertTrue(staging.pending(listOf(item)).isEmpty()) staging = staging.set(item) { it.copy(anonymous = true) }.remove(item, true) assertEquals(AO3CollectionItemDraft(item.id, remove = true), staging.shown(item)) staging = staging.remove(item, false) assertTrue(staging.pending(listOf(item)).isEmpty()) assertEquals(listOf(41, 43), AO3CollectionItemStaging(mapOf( 43 to AO3CollectionItemDraft(43, remove = true), 41 to AO3CollectionItemDraft(41, remove = true) )).pending(page().items).map { it.itemId }) }</code><br>android/app/src/test/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/account/AO3UserCollectionItemsTest.kt:182 <code>@Test fun onlyVisibleDraftsSubmitAndOtherPagesDraftsSurviveUntilDiscard() = runTest { val (_, client, model) = setup() model.load(); model.remove(model.state.value.page!!.items.first()) // 61 on page one val document = Jsoup.parse(client.html) document.selectFirst(&quot;h4#collection_item_61&quot;)!!.closest(&quot;li.collection.item&quot;)!!.remove() client.html = document.outerHtml() model.load(AO3CollectionItemTab.Approved, 2) assertTrue(model.state.value.pending.isEmpty()) model.remove(model.state.value.page!!.items.first { it.id == 64 }) model.confirmSubmit() assertEquals(listOf(64), client.posts.map(::userItemId)) assertEquals(setOf(61), model.state.value.staging.drafts.keys) model.discard() assertTrue(model.state.value.staging.drafts.isEmpty())</code> |

| <code>theDefaultLeavesAO3sOwnOrderAlone</code><br>The Default Leaves A O3s Own Order Alone. | KudosTests/AO3CollectionsFilterTests.swift:40 <code>#expect(names(AO3CollectionsFilter(), input) == [&quot;c&quot;, &quot;a&quot;, &quot;b&quot;])</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/account/AO3CollectionsFilter.kt:50 <code>fun apply(collections: List&lt;AO3Collection&gt;): List&lt;AO3Collection&gt; { val narrowed = collections.filter { (!showsOpenOnly &#124;&#124; !it.isClosed) &amp;&amp; (!showsUnrevealedOnly &#124;&#124; it.isUnrevealed) &amp;&amp; (!showsModeratedOnly &#124;&#124; it.isModerated) &amp;&amp; (!showsWithWorksOnly &#124;&#124; (it.worksCount ?: 0) != 0) } if (sort == Sort.AsReturned) return narrowed val collator = Collator.getInstance().apply { strength = Collator.SECONDARY } // sortedWith is stable: ties and unknown ranks retain incoming AO3 order. return narrowed.sortedWith { left, right -&gt; if (sort == Sort.Title) { val comparison = collator.compare(left.title, right.title) if (order == Order.Ascending) comparison else -comparison } else { val a = rank(left) val b = rank(right) when { a == null &amp;&amp; b == null -&gt; 0 a == null -&gt; 1 b == null -&gt; -1 order == Order.Ascending -&gt; a.compareTo(b) else -&gt; b.compareTo(a) } } } }  private fun rank(collection: AO3Collection): Long? = when (sort) { Sort.AsReturned, Sort.Title -&gt; null Sort.Works -&gt; collection.worksCount?.toLong() Sort.Bookmarks -&gt; collection.bookmarksCount?.toLong() Sort.RecentlyUpdated -&gt; updatedDate(collection.updatedAtText)?.toEpochDay()</code><br>android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/account/AO3CollectionsFilter.kt:86 <code>private val dateFormatters = listOf(&quot;d MMM uuuu&quot;, &quot;dd MMM uuuu&quot;, &quot;uuuu-MM-dd&quot;, &quot;d MMMM uuuu&quot;) .map { DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern(it, Locale.US).withResolverStyle(ResolverStyle.STRICT) }  /** Only recognised AO3 shapes; locale and the device time zone cannot reinterpret them. */ fun updatedDate(text: String): LocalDate? { val trimmed = text.trim() if (trimmed.isEmpty()) return null for (formatter in dateFormatters) { try { return LocalDate.parse(trimmed, formatter) } catch (_: DateTimeParseException) { // Unrecognised text remains unranked. } } return null</code><br>Default leaves order; filters intersect; stable sorting puts unknown counts/dates last without inventing zero, and only recognized dates get ranks. |

| <code>oldScopeMetadataEnrichmentCannotReachANewSession</code><br>Old Scope Metadata Enrichment Cannot Reach A New Session. | KudosTests/AO3InboxAccountTransitionTests.swift:454 <code>#expect(model.workContext(for: 7) == nil)</code> | **unsure** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/account/AccountInboxViewModel.kt:400 <code>private fun startWorkContextEnrichment(items: List&lt;AO3InboxItem&gt;) { enrichmentJob?.cancel() val workIds = items.mapNotNull { it.workId }.distinct() if (workIds.isEmpty()) return enrichmentJob = viewModelScope.launch { for (workId in workIds) { if (mutableState.value.workAuthorsById.containsKey(workId) &#124;&#124; workId in attemptedWorkIds) continue // Remember the attempt before the read, including a failed or cancelled one. attemptedWorkIds.add(workId) try { val canContinue = coordinator.coordinate { when ( val result = commentRepository.loadThread( AO3CommentTarget.Work(workId), useCache = false ) ) { is AO3Result.Success -&gt; { val authors = result.value.workAuthors mutableState.update { state -&gt; state.copy( workAuthorsById = state.workAuthorsById + (workId to authors) ) } true } is AO3Result.Failure -&gt; { // One unavailable work does not block the rest; a systemic // failure stops the batch rather than spending more reads. result.error == AO3Error.NotFound &#124;&#124; result.error == AO3Error.Forbidden } } } if (!canContinue) return@launch } catch (_: CancellationException) { return@launch</code><br>Cancellation on session change is present, but enrichment publishes without an explicit captured-viewer check and the destination’s full work-context contract is not traced. Do not claim that an actual cross-account completion is established solely from this block. |

The selected pill mislabels the rows shown. |

result.error != AO3Error.AuthenticationRequired) { it.copy(phase = AccountInboxUiState.Phase.Loaded, failedPage = page, failedPageMessage = result.error.displayMessage()) } else it.copy(phase = AccountInboxUiState.Phase.Failed, items = emptyList(), bulkForm = null, filterForm = null, isShowingStaleCache = false, actionError = result.error.displayMessage())</code><br>Failed page turns keep current items/page and report failedPage separately.<br>Android test (static evidence; not run): android/app/src/test/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/account/InboxFailedPageTest.kt:57 <code>@Test fun aFailedPageTurnKeepsThePageAndSaysWhichPageFailedAndASessionThatEndedKeepsNothing() = runTest { val client = Client() val model = AccountInboxViewModel(AO3InboxRepository(client, pageCache = AO3PageCache()), AO3CommentRepository(client, client)).also { this@InboxFailedPageTest.model = it } model.load(1)!!.join() val first = model.uiState.value assertTrue(first.items.isNotEmpty())  // Page 2 was never read, so there is no kept copy to fall back on: a true failure. client.failing = AO3Error.Server(500) model.goToPage(2) var now = model.uiState.value assertEquals(&quot;page 1 is still what is shown&quot;, first.items, now.items) assertEquals(AccountInboxUiState.Phase.Loaded, now.phase) assertEquals(1, now.currentPage) assertEquals(2, now.failedPage) assertNull(&quot;no dialog as well as the card&quot;, now.actionError)</code> |
| <code>retryAfterAFailedPage2RequestTargetsPage2NotPage1</code><br>Retry After A Failed Page2 Request Targets Page2 Not Page1. | KudosTests/AO3InboxPaginationFailureTests.swift:162 <code>#expect(model.phase == .loaded)</code><br>KudosTests/AO3InboxPaginationFailureTests.swift:165 <code>#expect(requestedPages.last?.contains(&quot;page=2&quot;) == true)</code><br>KudosTests/AO3InboxPaginationFailureTests.swift:166 <code>#expect(model.currentPage == 2)</code><br>KudosTests/AO3InboxPaginationFailureTests.swift:167 <code>#expect(model.totalComments == 20)</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/account/AccountInboxPane.kt:359 <code>state.failedPage?.let { failed -&gt; item { ErrorStateCard( title = &quot;Couldn&#x27;t load page $failed&quot;, message = state.failedPageMessage ?: &quot;Something went wrong.&quot;, modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 12.dp), primaryActionLabel = &quot;Try Again&quot;, onPrimaryAction = { viewModel.goToPage(failed) } ) }</code><br>The failed-page card’s retry uses the captured failed page; generic retry() is not the page-error button.<br>Android test (static evidence; not run): android/app/src/test/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/account/InboxFailedPageTest.kt:69 <code>assertEquals(&quot;page 1 is still what is shown&quot;, first.items, now.items) assertEquals(AccountInboxUiState.Phase.Loaded, now.phase) assertEquals(1, now.currentPage) assertEquals(2, now.failedPage) assertNull(&quot;no dialog as well as the card&quot;, now.actionError)  // Try Again, with AO3 answering: the card goes. client.failing = null model.goToPage(2) assertNull(model.uiState.value.failedPage)</code> |

| <code>aLoadedPageSelectsEverySparseRow</code><br>A Loaded Page Selects Every Sparse Row. | KudosTests/AO3SubscriptionsScreenTests.swift:240 <code>#expect(AO3SubscriptionsPageEnrichment.sparseWorks(in: page).map(\.id) == Array(1...20))</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/account/AccountWorksListScreen.kt:199 <code>// iOS enriches only the loaded subscriptions page, after it is on screen. Reuse the // rows&#x27; memoised anonymous lookup, sequentially; changing page/session cancels the walk. LaunchedEffect(type, pageWorks, authState, subscriptionsScope) { if (type == AccountListType.Subscriptions &amp;&amp; subscriptionsScope == &quot;works&quot; &amp;&amp; authState.isSignedIn) { for (work in pageWorks) { val known = enricher?.enrich(work) ?: work currentCoroutineContext().ensureActive() enrichedSubscriptions = enrichedSubscriptions + (work.id to known) if (known.hasPostedChapterCount()) { subscriptionWatermarks = SubscriptionWatermarks.baseline( context, SubscriptionWatermarks.NAMESPACE_SUBSCRIPTIONS, listOf(known) ) } } }</code><br>The page effect walks every loaded row, including offscreen ones, and stores enriched summaries; known posted counts are baselined. |

state.counts[AccountListType.History.listKey]?.displayText, onClick = { onOpenList(AccountListType.History) } ) HorizontalDivider(modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 50.dp)) AccountScopeRow( title = &quot;Inbox&quot;, icon = Icons.Outlined.Inbox, onClick = onOpenInbox</code><br>Reading offers all four rows, Writing all three including Drafts, and Activity History/Inbox, in the tested order. |

## CollectionModerationCopyTests

| Test and reader guarantee | iOS assertion | Verdict | Android evidence and consequence |
|---|---|---|---|
| <code>declineConfirmationNamesTheParticipant</code><br>Decline Confirmation Names The Participant. | KudosTests/CollectionModerationCopyTests.swift:9 <code>#expect(CollectionModerationCopy.declineTitle(participant: &quot;WriterName (alice)&quot;) == &quot;Decline WriterName (alice)?&quot;)</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/account/AO3CollectionModerationState.kt:39 <code>internal data class ModerationDecision(val action: ModerationAction, val id: Int = 0, val name: String = &quot;&quot;) { val title get() = when (action) { ModerationAction.Reject -&gt; &quot;Reject this work?&quot; ModerationAction.Decline -&gt; &quot;Decline $name?&quot; ModerationAction.Reveal -&gt; &quot;Reveal this collection?&quot; ModerationAction.Unanon -&gt; &quot;Remove anonymity?&quot;</code><br>android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/account/AO3CollectionModerationScreen.kt:208 <code>ModerationPill(&quot;Decline&quot;, palette, enabled = !state.busy) { scope.launch { model.choose(ModerationDecision(ModerationAction.Decline, participant.id, participant.pseud)) }</code><br>Decline confirmation carries the tapped participant’s displayed name. |
| <code>recentlyDecidedStartsOnApproved</code><br>Recently Decided Starts On Approved. | KudosTests/CollectionModerationCopyTests.swift:14 <code>#expect(AO3CollectionItemsView.startingTab(slug: &quot;fest&quot;, initialTab: .approved) == .approved)</code><br>KudosTests/CollectionModerationCopyTests.swift:15 <code>#expect(AO3CollectionItemsView.startingTab(slug: &quot;fest&quot;, initialTab: nil) == .unreviewed)</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/account/AO3CollectionItemsScreen.kt:89 <code>initialTab: AO3CollectionItemTab? = null ) { val auth = repository.authRepository val generation by auth.generation.collectAsState() val authState by auth.state.collectAsState() // Key the state on auth before rendering: replacement sessions never see old private rows/forms. val model = remember(slug, generation, authState.isSignedIn) { AO3CollectionItemsState(slug, repository, writes) } val state by model.state.collectAsState() val defaultTab = AO3CollectionItemTab.defaultTab(slug) // The scope&#x27;s own default, unless the caller names a tab (Moderation opens Approved). var tab by remember(slug, initialTab) { mutableStateOf(initialTab ?: defaultTab) }</code><br>An explicit Approved initialTab overrides the collection’s normal Unreviewed start. |
| <code>reviewTallyLabelsAPagedQueueAsThisPage</code><br>Review Tally Labels A Paged Queue As This Page. | KudosTests/CollectionModerationCopyTests.swift:19 <code>#expect(CollectionModerationCopy.reviewTally(count: 4, page: 1, totalPages: 1) == &quot;4 works awaiting review&quot;)</code><br>KudosTests/CollectionModerationCopyTests.swift:20 <code>#expect(CollectionModerationCopy.reviewTally(count: 1, page: 1, totalPages: 1) == &quot;1 work awaiting review&quot;)</code><br>KudosTests/CollectionModerationCopyTests.swift:21 <code>#expect(CollectionModerationCopy.reviewTally(count: 20, page: 2, totalPages: 3) == &quot;20 works awaiting review on this page · page 2 of 3&quot;)</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/account/AO3CollectionModerationScreen.kt:104 <code>val queue = state.data?.queue val count = queue?.items?.size ?: 0 val works = if (count == 1) &quot;1 work awaiting review&quot; else &quot;$count works awaiting review&quot; val paged = if (queue != null &amp;&amp; queue.totalPages &gt; 1) &quot; on this page · page ${queue.currentPage} of ${queue.totalPages}&quot; else &quot;&quot;</code><br>Tally says “on this page” and includes real page numbers for a paged queue. |
| <code>anEmptiedPageMovesToTheNearestPageAO3StillHas</code><br>An Emptied Page Moves To The Nearest Page A O3 Still Has. | KudosTests/CollectionModerationCopyTests.swift:27 <code>#expect(CollectionModerationCopy.pageAfterEmptying(page: 2, totalPages: 3) == 2)</code><br>KudosTests/CollectionModerationCopyTests.swift:28 <code>#expect(CollectionModerationCopy.pageAfterEmptying(page: 3, totalPages: 3) == 2)</code><br>KudosTests/CollectionModerationCopyTests.swift:29 <code>#expect(CollectionModerationCopy.pageAfterEmptying(page: 1, totalPages: 1) == 1)</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/account/AO3CollectionModerationState.kt:177 <code>ModerationAction.Approve, ModerationAction.Reject -&gt; { val queue = data.queue.copy(items = data.queue.items.filterNot { it.id == decision.id }) mutable.value = state.value.copy(data = data.copy(queue = queue)) if (queue.items.isEmpty() &amp;&amp; queue.totalPages &gt; 1) loadPage( if (queue.currentPage &lt; queue.totalPages) queue.currentPage else maxOf(1, queue.currentPage - 1))</code><br>After the last visible row is removed, keep a still-existing page or step back from the final page. |
| <code>bylineAddsTheSubmissionDateOnlyWhenAO3PrintedOne</code><br>Byline Adds The Submission Date Only When A O3 Printed One. | KudosTests/CollectionModerationCopyTests.swift:33 <code>#expect(CollectionModerationCopy.byline(creator: &quot;kestrelmoon&quot;, dateText: &quot;03 Nov 2026&quot;) == &quot;kestrelmoon · submitted 03 Nov 2026&quot;)</code><br>KudosTests/CollectionModerationCopyTests.swift:35 <code>#expect(CollectionModerationCopy.byline(creator: &quot;kestrelmoon&quot;, dateText: &quot; &quot;) == &quot;kestrelmoon&quot;)</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/account/AO3CollectionModerationScreen.kt:156 <code>Text(work.creatorByline + work.dateText.trim().takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() } ?.let { &quot; · submitted $it&quot; }.orEmpty(), color = tokens.secondaryInk, fontSize = 11.5.sp, lineHeight = 17.sp)</code><br>Blank date adds nothing; a real date is prefixed submitted. |
| <code>aRejectionPostsOnlyTheApprovalField</code><br>A Rejection Posts Only The Approval Field. | KudosTests/CollectionModerationCopyTests.swift:43 <code>#expect(params.map(\.0) == [&quot;authenticity_token&quot;, &quot;_method&quot;, &quot;collection_items[7][collection_approval_status]&quot;])</code><br>KudosTests/CollectionModerationCopyTests.swift:44 <code>#expect(params.last?.1 == &quot;rejected&quot;)</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/account/AO3CollectionModerationState.kt:155 <code>val result = when (decision.action) { ModerationAction.Approve, ModerationAction.Reject -&gt; writes.updateCollectionItems(slug, listOf(AO3CollectionItemDraft(decision.id, moderatorApproval = if (decision.action == ModerationAction.Approve) AO3CollectionItemApproval.Approved else AO3CollectionItemApproval.Rejected)), generation)</code><br>android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/account/AO3CollectionItems.kt:89 <code>fun parameters(page: AO3CollectionItemsPage): List&lt;Pair&lt;String, String&gt;&gt; = buildList { add(&quot;authenticity_token&quot; to page.csrfToken) add(&quot;_method&quot; to (page.methodOverride ?: &quot;patch&quot;)) val prefix = &quot;collection_items[$itemId]&quot; if (remove) { add(&quot;$prefix[remove]&quot; to &quot;1&quot;) } else { creatorApproval?.let { add(&quot;$prefix[user_approval_status]&quot; to it.value) } moderatorApproval?.let { add(&quot;$prefix[collection_approval_status]&quot; to it.value) } unrevealed?.let { add(&quot;$prefix[unrevealed]&quot; to if (it) &quot;1&quot; else &quot;0&quot;) } anonymous?.let { add(&quot;$prefix[anonymous]&quot; to if (it) &quot;1&quot; else &quot;0&quot;) } }</code><br>Rejection draft has only moderatorApproval, so visibility/anonymity/creator state are not changed. |
| <code>theReviewQueueKeepsAO3sPageCount</code><br>The Review Queue Keeps A O3s Page Count. | KudosTests/CollectionModerationCopyTests.swift:73 <code>#expect(page.currentPage == 2)</code><br>KudosTests/CollectionModerationCopyTests.swift:74 <code>#expect(page.totalPages == 3)</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/account/AO3CollectionItems.kt:169 <code>val total = doc.select(&quot;ol.pagination li&quot;).mapNotNull { it.text().toIntOrNull() } .fold(page) { count, number -&gt; maxOf(count, number) } return AO3CollectionItemsPage(items, tab, page, total, action, csrf, form?.selectFirst(&quot;input[name=_method]&quot;)?.attr(&quot;value&quot;)?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() })</code><br>Parses pagination into the queue’s actual totalPages rather than substituting one. |

|---|---|---|---|
| <code>parsePromptMemeRequestsFixtureReturns4Prompts</code><br>Parse Prompt Meme Requests Fixture Returns4 Prompts. | KudosTests/PromptMemeFixtureTests.swift:16 <code>#expect(page.prompts.count == 4)</code> | **unsure** | No Android counterpart has yet been established for the entire assertion; no kept/missing claim without a Kotlin quote. |
| <code>theCloseDateIsAskedForOnceAndOnlyForAnOwner</code><br>The Close Date Is Asked For Once And Only For An Owner. | KudosTests/PromptMemeFixtureTests.swift:24 <code>#expect(PromptMemeView.readsSchedule(viewerIsOwner: true, attempted: false, hasDate: false))</code><br>KudosTests/PromptMemeFixtureTests.swift:26 <code>#expect(!PromptMemeView.readsSchedule(viewerIsOwner: true, attempted: true, hasDate: false))</code><br>KudosTests/PromptMemeFixtureTests.swift:27 <code>#expect(!PromptMemeView.readsSchedule(viewerIsOwner: true, attempted: true, hasDate: true))</code><br>KudosTests/PromptMemeFixtureTests.swift:29 <code>#expect(!PromptMemeView.readsSchedule(viewerIsOwner: true, attempted: false, hasDate: true))</code><br>KudosTests/PromptMemeFixtureTests.swift:31 <code>#expect(!PromptMemeView.readsSchedule(viewerIsOwner: false, attempted: false, hasDate: false))</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/account/AO3PromptMemeState.kt:29 <code>internal fun readsPromptMemeSchedule(viewerIsOwner: Boolean, attempted: Boolean, hasDate: Boolean): Boolean = viewerIsOwner &amp;&amp; !attempted &amp;&amp; !hasDate</code><br>Requires owner, no previous attempt and no known date; failed attempts do not trigger another automatic settings read. |

## OmittedFieldKeepsPreferenceTests
